### [109] [Incidence Relations for Self-Dual Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07774)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了自对偶黑洞时空（如Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT和自对偶Plebański-Demiański）在Kerr-Schild坐标下的扭量空间入射关系的具体局部构造，基于Plebański第二天堂方程与Dunajski-Mason递归方法，得到了闭式解，并发现自对偶Taub-NUT的入射关系线性依赖于引力耦合常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解自对偶时空的几何结构及其扭量描述，需具体实现Penrose非线性引力子构造中的入射关系，尤其针对重要的自对偶黑洞解。

Method: 采用Plebański第二天堂方程框架，结合Dunajski-Mason递归方法，显式求解Plebański标量，从而导出各类自对偶黑洞时空的入射关系。

Result: 得到了Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT及自对偶Plebański-Demiański时空的闭式入射关系；特别地，自对偶Taub-NUT的入射关系显式线性依赖于引力耦合；并简要拓展至零静止质量场的构造。

Conclusion: 该工作为自对偶引力与扭量理论提供了具体的可计算范例，强化了扭量空间与自对偶黑洞几何之间的联系，并为后续量子化及散射问题研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In Penrose's nonlinear graviton construction, a self-dual spacetime arises as the moduli space of holomorphic twistor lines in curved twistor space, whose explicit parametrization is concretely given by the incidence relation. We demonstrate a concrete local construction of the incidence relations for self-dual black hole spacetimes in Kerr-Schild coordinates: Eguchi-Hanson, self-dual Taub-NUT, and self-dual Plebański-Demiański. This is facilitated by implementing the Dunajski-Mason recursion in the framework of Plebański's second heavenly equation, for which we explicitly find the Plebański scalar. Our results describe closed-form formulae. Notably, for the self-dual Taub-NUT solution, the exact incidence relation exhibits linear dependence on the gravitational coupling. The construction of zero-rest-mass fields on self-dual black hole backgrounds is briefly sketched as an application.

</details>


### [110] [Four-Fermion Condensates in Curved Spacetimes: A Functional Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07907)
*Stephon Alexander,Heliudson Bernardo,Jiatong Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了四费米子相互作用在不同物理场景（如微扰散射、NJL真空不稳定性、BCS配对）中的区分特征，并首次在弯曲时空背景下计算了标量通道NJL平均场对能量-动量张量的一圈贡献，推导出协变量子有效作用量及对应的能量密度与压强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四费米子相互作用广泛出现在粒子物理、多体系统和含费米子的引力理论中，但其在不同物理机制（如NJL不稳定性与BCS配对）中对应不同相互作用通道、二次核与量子态，亟需统一的功能性描述框架。

Method: 采用路径积分方法，构建in-out与闭时路径（CTP）泛函以处理非平衡期望值；对NJL鞍点引入Hubbard-Stratonovich辅助场，计算费米子行列式的一圈修正，提取曲率相关的局域项，并在FLRW背景下推导能量密度与压强。

Result: 得到弯曲时空中标量通道NJL平均场贡献的协变量子有效作用量，包含体积、曲率及曲率平方项；导出对应能量密度与压强表达式，常凝聚极限下与平直空间结果一致；明确了BCS配对所需额外的状态与通道信息，并讨论了弯曲时空中的重整化条件。

Conclusion: 本文为理解四费米子相互作用在不同物理语境下的共性与差异提供了统一功能框架，拓展了NJL型机制至弯曲时空，为早期宇宙相变、致密星体等强耦合弯曲时空费米系统建模奠定基础。

Abstract: Four-fermion interactions appear in effective descriptions of particle physics, many-body systems, and gravitational theories with fermions. Although the same local operator may enter perturbative scattering, a vacuum Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) instability, or finite-density Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) pairing, these regimes are distinguished by their interaction channels, quadratic kernels, and quantum states. We give a pedagogical functional account of these distinctions and compute the local one-loop contribution of a scalar-channel NJL mean field to the energy-momentum tensor in curved spacetime. We first display the state data in the in-out functional and construct the closed-time-path functional required for an in-in expectation value. For the NJL saddle, a Hubbard-Stratonovich field shifts the fermion mass, and the parity-even Dirac determinant generates local volume, curvature, and curvature-squared operators. We find the covariant quantum effective action before specializing to a spatially flat FLRW background and derive the corresponding energy density and pressure. The constant-condensate limit agrees with the direct flat-space mean-field calculation. We also explain which additional state and channel data are required for finite-density BCS pairing and comment on renormalization conditions in curved spacetimes.

</details>


### [111] [One gravity theory on the two-dimensional p-adic space and conjectures on $p$-adic AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07919)
*Feng Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过高维嵌入方法，结合距离、测度和曲率的研究，在p进数上的二维空间构建了一种引力理论，并通过与实数情形的对比验证其合理性，最后提出了两个关于p进AdS/CFT和p进CFT的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建p进数上二维空间的引力理论，并验证其与实数情形的一致性。

Method: 采用高维嵌入方法，研究距离、测度和曲率，并与实数情形（如dS、AdS、欧氏空间的曲率）进行对比验证。

Result: 成功构建了p进数上二维空间的引力理论，并提出两个猜想：(1) p进AdS/CFT可在任意空间计算且结果一致；(2) p进CFT本质上唯一。

Conclusion: 该引力理论在p进数框架下具有合理性，所提出的猜想为后续p进量子引力与对偶性研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: By means of a higher-dimensional embedding approach, together with the study of distance, measure, and curvature, we construct a gravitational theory on the two-dimensional space over $p$-adic numbers. Before obtaining the final gravitational theory, we supplement some calculations based on existing literature and justify the reasonableness of the gravitational theory by comparison with the real-number case-for instance, the curvatures of de Sitter(dS), anti-de Sitter(AdS), and Euclidean spaces should be positive, negative, and zero, respectively. Finally, we put forward two conjectures: (1) $p$-adic AdS/CFT can be computed in arbitrary spaces, not only in $p$-adic AdS space($p$AdS), and the results are essentially the same; (2) $p$-adic CFT is essentially unique.

</details>


### [112] [Gauge transformations in Z-space in (anti)holomorphic sector of HS theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08062)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立与Vasiliev理论(反)全纯截断的映射，需对原有生成系统进行相容形变，并理解主场Λ的灵活性及其对场论结构的影响。

Method: 引入dz-恰当射影1-形式对主场Λ进行平移，系统分类射影1-形式并证明相关射影恒等式；推导n阶C依赖的dz-恰当形式所诱导的(n+1)阶及以上场重定义；分析零形式中C二阶的规范变换，判定其中可被规范掉的部分。

Result: 给出了(n+1)阶场重定义的显式表达式；明确了C二阶场重定义中可规范掉的充要条件；严格证明了由Λ平移诱导的场重定义是非平凡的。

Conclusion: 该形变方案不仅增强了Λ场的几何自由度，而且揭示了高阶场重定义的结构性质与不可消去性，为深入理解Vasiliev理论的(反)全纯结构提供了新路径。

Abstract: We consider a consistent deformation of the (anti)holomorphic generating system of [arXiv:2209.01966], aiming to provide a map to the (anti)holomorphic truncation of the Vasiliev theory. In our new formulation, the previously rigidly defined master-field $Λ$ can be shifted by $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact projective one-forms. The class of projective one-forms is described comprehensively, and corresponding projective identities are proven. $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact forms of the order $n$ in $C$ induce field redefinition of order $(n+1)$ and beyond. Explicit expression for the $(n+1)$-th order field redefinitions are provided. An analysis of the gauge transformation of the second order in $C$ in zero-forms is performed, identifying necessary and sufficient conditions for the part of the field redefinition of the second order that can be gauged away. The field redefinitions induced by the shift of $Λ$ are proven to be nontrivial.

</details>


### [113] [Complex CFTs: Holography and Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08292)
*Luis Camargo-Carlos,Michael Gutperle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全息方法和二维共形场论（CFT）研究复共形场理论及其界面，证明了复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质，构造了复Janus解与复RG流界面，并在自由玻色子CFT中构建了带/不带背景电荷的复界面及相应边界态，计算了透射与反射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复共形场理论及其界面的结构与物理性质，特别是借助全息对偶探索复CFT中的非平凡对称性（如Im-flip）及界面动力学。

Method: 结合AdS/CFT全息对偶与二维CFT技术：利用全息构造复Janus解和数值求解复RG流界面；在CFT侧显式构造自由玻色子模型的复界面、边界态，并计算传输/反射系数。

Result: 证明了复共轭CFT满足Im-flip性质；给出了复Janus解及其全息观测量；获得数值复RG流界面；构建了两类自由玻色子复界面的边界态，并得到对应的透射与反射系数。

Conclusion: 复CFT及其界面具有丰富的结构，Im-flip是其基本对称性；全息与CFT方法可协同揭示复界面的几何与量子特性，为理解非幺正或PT对称量子场论提供新路径。

Abstract: We investigate complex conformal field theories and interfaces between such theories, using methods of holography and two dimensional CFT. We use holography to prove the Im-flip property of complex conjugate CFTs. We construct a complex Janus solution and calculate some holographic observables. Complex RG-flow interfaces are obtained numerically. On the CFT side, complex interfaces are constructed for free boson CFTs with and without background charge. Boundary states are constructed, and transmission and reflection coefficients are calculated in both cases.

</details>


### [114] [Carrollian Quantum Mechanics: Time-like, Space-like and Hybrid Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08384)
*Mehdi Ahmadi-Jahmani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了Carrollian量子力学的理论框架，通过对Klein-Gordon方程在c→0极限下的系统超相对论收缩，得到三个Carroll不变的波方程分支（类时、类空与混合），并分别给出其概率诠释、连续性方程及典型量子现象分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Carroll对称性下的量子力学理论框架，弥补非相对论（伽利略）与超相对论（Carroll）极限之间理论空白，并探索其独特的物理现象（如时间隧穿、零范数态等）。

Method: 对Klein-Gordon方程实施系统性的c→0超相对论收缩，识别并分类出时间类、空间类和混合三类Carroll不变波方程；推导各对应连续性方程、概率密度与流；分析典型量子系统（如无限深势阱、隧穿）。

Result: 发现三类Carroll不变波方程及相应概率诠释：类时分支呈现时间隧穿（|T|² = 1 + |R|²）；类空分支要求快子色散关系，导致能量本征态密度为零（零范数态）；混合分支具双重形式（快子/非快子），可解释为非齐次KG方程。

Conclusion: Carrollian量子力学具有丰富而独特的结构，三类分支各自对应不同几何与物理内涵，零范数态与时间隧穿等现象凸显Carroll对称性下量子理论的本质差异，为理解平直时空边界与极端相对论极限提供新视角。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive theoretical framework for Carrollian quantum mechanics by performing systematic ultra-relativistic contractions of the Klein--Gordon equation in the limit $c \to 0$. This limiting process uncovers three distinct sectors---time-like, space-like, and hybrid---each governed by a Carroll-invariant wave equation and accompanied by a consistent probabilistic interpretation. The time-like sector exhibits a novel temporal tunneling phenomenon, characterized by the relation $|\mathcal{T}|^2 = 1 + |\mathcal{R}|^2$, which reflects the indefinite character of the Klein--Gordon norm. In the space-like sector, the presence of spatial propagation necessitates a tachyonic dispersion relation, which forces the density to vanish for energy eigenstates, yielding zero-norm (null) states that gain physical relevance within Carrollian physics due to the underlying null geometric structure. The hybrid sector combines features of both sectors and admits two distinct formulations, which may be either tachyonic or non-tachyonic, and can be interpreted as an inhomogeneous Klein--Gordon equation. For all three sectors, we derive the corresponding continuity equations, probability densities, and currents, and investigate canonical quantum systems---including the particle in a box and tunneling phenomena---within the Carrollian regime.

</details>


### [115] [Organizing transitions and their cascades: Generalized symmetry enforcement in massless flows or Higgs transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08428)
*Yoshiki Fukusumi,Yuma Furuta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究广义对称性在无质量重整化群流（如Higgs相变）中的作用，发现SU(2)_{p-2}融合环对称性可完全消除红外理论中的相关扰动，从而稳定无能隙相，并揭示其与非阿贝尔任意子构成的类Cooper对（半整数/整数自旋非简单流）之间的联系及其在共振效应和级联相变中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义对称性（特别是融合环对称性）如何稳定无质量重整化群流及无能隙相，拓展对称性保护临界现象的理论框架。

Method: 基于单位模最小模型M(p,p+1)→M̅(p−1,p)的质量less流，分析FR(SU(2)_{p−2})对称性的保持及其对相关扰动的禁阻；结合折叠技巧、coset与level-rank对偶结构，阐释非简单流的物理实现及共振机制。

Result: 证明FR(SU(2)_{p−2})对称性完全消除红外模型中的所有相关扰动，确立M̅(p−1,p)为弱对称性强制的无能隙相；识别出对应于半整数/整数自旋非简单流的物理图像，并揭示其引发相关与危险无关扰动共振、进而导致级联相变或流向未知不动点的机制。

Conclusion: 融合环对称性不仅是稳定无能隙相的关键保护机制，还可作为抑制非常规相变流的‘对称性刹车’，为强关联系统中广义对称性与临界行为的关系提供新范式。

Abstract: We study the role of generalized symmetry in massless renormalization group flows or Higgs transitions. In particular, we revisit the massless renormalization group flows in unitary minimal models, $\mathbf{M}(p,p+1) \rightarrow \overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ preserving the fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})\subset \mathbf{M}(p,p+1)$ where $p$ is an integer satisfying $p>3$. In this series of flows, we demonstrate that the unbroken fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})$ eliminates all relevant perturbations in the $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ model. Hence, the infrared theory $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ is stable at the level of the scaling analysis and can be interpreted as a (weak-)symmetry-enforced gapless phase in contemporary theoretical physics. Phenomenologically, by the folding trick, the unbroken fusion ring symmetry corresponds to a (half-)integer spin nonsimple current, a variant of the Cooper pair involving nonabelian anyons generated from the coset or level-rank duality structures. Moreover, we demonstrate that the structure of (half-)integer spin nonsimple current plays a fundamental role in causing the resonance effect of relevant and dangerously irrelevant perturbations. This resonance effect may result in the cascade of phase transitions (or the system flows to unexplored fixed points), and the symmetry can be a stopper of such unconventional flows.

</details>


### [116] [Conformally mapping HKLL to flat space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08593)
*Nirmalya Kajuri,Rhitaparna Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过共形映射研究了AdS₃中共形耦合标量场的HKLL重构问题，将其转化为半爱因斯坦静态宇宙及平直时空中的加速圆形狄利克雷镜面问题，并在平直空间中内在地求解了受谱类限制的镜面问题，同时发现了一个不满足运动方程却仍有效的涂抹函数，并分析了AdS-Rindler楔形重构中涂抹函数发散的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息对偶中边界数据如何重构体内的局部算符，特别是HKLL重建在不同几何背景下的表现及其限制条件。

Method: 利用共形变换将AdS₃上的HKLL问题依次映射到半爱因斯坦静态宇宙和(2+1)维闵氏时空中的加速圆形狄利克雷镜面区域，并在平直空间中直接求解受AdS正规模谱约束的镜面响应问题。

Result: 成功构造了一个满足HKLL要求但自身不满足运动方程的有效涂抹函数；明确了镜面边界数据必须属于由AdS正规模继承的受限谱类；揭示了AdS-Rindler楔形重构中涂抹函数发散的几何根源。

Conclusion: HKLL重构不仅依赖于几何结构，更受原始AdS模态谱性质的深层约束；共形映射为理解不同时空中的全息重建提供了新视角，并凸显了涂抹函数物理有效性与数学形式之间的微妙关系。

Abstract: We study two conformal images of the HKLL reconstruction problem for a conformally coupled scalar on AdS$_3$. The first mapping is to a Dirichlet problem on one half of the Einstein static universe. A further conformal transformation maps this half-ESU problem to a massless scalar in a region of $(2+1)$-dimensional Minkowski space bounded by an uniformly accelerated circular Dirichlet mirror. However, the global HKLL problem, the resulting flat-space problem is not a generic local mirror problem with arbitrary boundary data. Rather, the mirror response data must belong to a restricted spectral class inherited from normalizable global AdS modes. We formulate and solve this restricted mirror problem intrinsically in flat space. In the process, we find an example of a valid smearing function which itself does not satisfy the equations of motion. We further consider AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. By conformal mapping to an appropriate coordinate system in flat space, we find a simple way to see the blow-up of the smearing function.

</details>


### [117] [Thermodynamic stability and geometric thermodynamics of charged black holes in Rastall-massive gravity under quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08660)
*Mohamed Chabab,Samir Iraoui,Hicham Sriba*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出一个在Rastall理论与质量引力框架下、嵌入于宇宙学常数类（quintessence）场中的新带电黑洞解，并研究其热力学性质与相变行为，发现其热力学行为类似于RN-AdS黑洞和范德瓦尔斯流体，且光子球可观测量可作为热力学临界相变的间接观测标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在Rastall理论与质量引力结合框架下，带电黑洞在quintessence场中的新解及其热力学行为，特别是相变机制与可观测信号之间的联系。

Method: 构建含quintessence场的Rastall-质量引力模型，求解带电黑洞精确解；将quintessence参数视为热力学变量，分析热力学量（如热容、吉布斯自由能）、相变及光子球轨道；采用Quevedo热力学几何方法验证临界行为。

Result: 发现当quintessence状态参数ω_q = -2/3时，黑洞热力学表现出类似RN-AdS黑洞和范德瓦尔斯流体的临界相变；光子球半径等可观测量与热力学相变点精确对应；Quevedo几何分析结果与传统热力学分析一致。

Conclusion: quintessence场的有效贡献使该模型黑洞展现出丰富热力学相结构，光子球可观测量可作为探测黑洞热力学临界现象的新途径，且热力学几何方法为其提供了自洽的几何描述。

Abstract: In the present paper, we derive a new charged black hole solution embedded in a quintessence field within a framework combining the Rastall and massive gravity in an asymptotically flat spacetime. We interpret the integration constant associated with the structural properties of the quintessence field as a thermodynamical variable. In this context, several limiting cases have been explored with the aim to shed light on the role of the model parameters. Then, we analyse the thermodynamic properties and stability of the charged black holes in flat spacetime, where the quintessence field is characterized by the state parameter \( ω_q = -2/3 \). Detailed studies are perfomed to see how the various parameters affect phase transitions between small and large black holes. In particular, we establish a connection between the impact parameter and unstable circular photon orbits with the thermodynamic phase transitions. Our analysis suggest that the photon sphere observables could remarkably provide an indirect observational signature of black hole thermodynamic critical transitions. Generally, thanks to the effective contribution of the quintessence parameter, we found that the overall thermodynamic behavior basically resembles that of the charged RN--AdS black hole with thermal features analogous to of a Van der Waals fluid ones. Finally, we have checked these findings using thermodynamic geometry with the Quevedo metric. Our results showed that the critical behaviour derived from the standard thermodynamics is fully consistent with geothermodynamics description, where the phase structure and divergence points exactly coincide with those identified from heat capacity and Gibbs free energy analyses.

</details>


### [118] [An explicit four-corner dictionary for (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08669)
*A. Alexandrov,V. Belavin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究(2,p)最小Liouville引力的四种代数描述及其对偶关系，重点建立Frobenius流形与谱曲线/拓扑递归两种框架下y-侧与x-侧描述之间的显式对应，并通过色散零tau函数验证一致性，结果与共形场论三点半函数比一致，并复现带符号Verlinde矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示(2,p)最小Liouville引力中不同代数描述（Frobenius流形与谱曲线/拓扑递归）之间的内在统一性与对偶结构，特别是y-侧与x-侧描述的等价性及其物理含义。

Method: 基于Frobenius流形（A₁与A_{p−1}）和谱曲线/拓扑递归（Chebyshev曲线及其x↔y交换）两套框架，推导四类描述在色散零tau函数层面的显式对应；结合共振变换、广义Kontsevich积分变换及树级坐标变换分析；在(2,5)至(2,11)模型中进行数值验证。

Result: 确立了四种代数描述在色散零tau函数上的精确对应；三点半函数比在所有四类表述中均与共形场论一致；完整振幅匹配唯一每插入因子，复现出带符号Verlinde矩阵；x↔y交换被实现为广义Kontsevich变换的树级坐标变换。

Conclusion: 四种代数描述本质上统一，其差异源于变量选择与坐标变换；该统一性不仅体现于低阶关联函数，亦贯穿于全振幅结构，强化了Liouville引力、拓扑递归与矩阵模型间的深层联系。

Abstract: (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity admits four algebraic descriptions interrelated by dualities. On the Frobenius manifold (FM) side the theory is built either on the $A_{1}$ manifold of $Q(y)=y^{2}+u_{1}$ ($y$-side) or on the $A_{p-1}$ manifold in the variable $x$ ($x$-side). On the spectral curve / topological recursion (SC) side the analogous choice is the Chebyshev curve and its $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap. FM-$y$, SC-$y$ require a resonance transformation between the KdV times and the Liouville couplings, while the other two do not. We work out the explicit correspondence between the four approaches at the level of the dispersionless tau-function. The normalisation-independent three-point ratios agree with conformal field theory in all four formulations, and matching the full amplitudes fixes a single per-insertion factor reproducing the signed Verlinde matrices. The $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap relating the two spectral curve descriptions, recently established in general by Dekinga, Shadrin and Verlinde, is realised at genus zero as a tree-level frame change of the generalised Kontsevich integral transform with potential $Φ^{p}/p$. The derivation is validated across (2,5)--(2,11) models.

</details>


### [119] [The explicit Lorentz invariant QED pair production rates from superstrings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08712)
*J. X. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过开放弦对产生机制，研究了Type II超弦理论中两个平行Dp膜（1≤p≤6）系统中的Schwinger效应，并在场论极限下，系统导出不同维度下洛伦兹不变的QED粒子对产生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Schwinger效应的新途径，特别是通过开放弦对产生机制理解高维QED中的粒子对产生。

Method: 利用Type II超弦理论中带电磁背景的平行Dp膜系统，计算开放弦对产生率，并取场论极限以导出QED结果。

Result: 得到了不同维度下显式洛伦兹不变的QED粒子对产生率，适用于标量、旋量和矢量等带质量荷电/反荷电粒子。

Conclusion: 开放弦对产生可作为统一框架，系统导出场论中多维Schwinger效应的洛伦兹不变结果，揭示弦论与量子场论间的深层联系。

Abstract: We present a new avenue to the usual Schwinger effect via the open string pair production for a system of two Dp branes with $1 \le p \le 6$ in Type II superstrings. The two Dp are placed parallel at a separation with one of them carrying the most general constant electromagnetic background allowed for the pair production. By taking the so-called field theory limit, we obtain systematically, from the open string pair production rate, the respective \textit{explicit Lorentz invariant} QED pair production rate in diverse dimensions for a pair of charged/anti-charged massive particles, which can be scalars, spinors or vectors.

</details>


### [120] [Understanding Non-Split 2-Group Symmetry: (3+1)D SymTFT, Anomaly and Bordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08766)
*Zhenbang Gu,Ran Luo,Yi-Nan Wang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了具有非平凡Postnikov类的有限非分裂2-群对称性，聚焦于最简单的Z2 0-形与Z2 1-形对称性通过H³(BZ₂;Z₂)中非平凡类耦合的情形；计算了d≤5维时空下该2-群的反常（通过定向和自旋配边群），并在d=3时利用融合2范畴和(3+1)D TQFT刻画其对称性TFT/TO，分类了无/有2-群反常下的边界条件及范畴朗道范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有非平凡Postnikov类的非分裂有限2-群对称性及其在量子场论中的反常结构与对称性实现方式，特别是低维（d≤5）下的拓扑反常分类与边界物理。

Method: 使用定向配边群Ω_{d+1}^{SO}(B𝒢)和自旋配边群Ω_{d+1}^{Spin}(B𝒢)计算d维理论的2-群反常；对d=3情形，采用融合2范畴语言与(3+1)D拓扑量子场论（TQFT）动作，结合上同调H⁴(B𝒢;U(1))≅Z₂刻画反常，并分类对称性TFT的最小拓扑/物理边界条件及范畴朗道序。

Result: 完整分类了d≤5维下该Z₂-Z₂非分裂2-群𝒢的反常；对d=3情形，明确给出其对称性TFT/TO的融合2范畴描述、无/有反常对应的TQFT动作，以及边界条件的范畴分类与朗道范式实现。

Conclusion: 非分裂2-群对称性由Postnikov类编码的高阶结构深刻影响其反常与边界物理，融合2范畴与配边理论是刻画此类广义对称性的有效数学框架；结果为高阶对称性在凝聚态与高能物理中的应用提供基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of finite, non-split 2-group symmetries with a non-trivial Postnikov class, focusing on the simplest example with a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 0-form symmetry and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 1-form symmetry, intertwined together by the non-trivial Postnikov class in $H^3(B\mathbb{Z}_2;\mathbb{Z}_2)\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$, denoted by $\mathcal{G}$. We classify the anomalies of this 2-group symmetry for physical theories in $d$-dimensional spacetime, by computing the oriented bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G})$ and the spin bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm Spin}(B\mathcal{G})$ for $d\leq 5$. For the case of $d=3$, we investigate the Symmetry TFT/TO of the 2-group symmetry $\mathcal{G}$ using the language of fusion 2-categories, as well as (3+1)D TQFT actions, for the cases without or with the 2-group anomaly classified by $\operatorname{Hom}\left(\widetildeΩ_4^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G}),U(1)\right) \cong H^4(B\mathcal{G};U(1))\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$. We classify the minimal topological and physical boundary conditions of the Symmetry TFTs, and carry out the categorical Landau paradigm for such non-split finite 2-group.

</details>


### [121] [3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror symmetry, TQFT interfaces, and Zagier duality of Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08780)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和出发，构造了两个三维$\mathcal{N}=2$ Chern-Simons物质理论$\mathcal{T}$与$\mathcal{T}^\vee$，通过A/B-twisted半指数、镜像对称、Zagier对偶及TQFT数据匹配，揭示了其与$E_8$模形式、真空字符及rank-zero $\mathcal{N}=4$ SCFT之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立三维超对称场论与模形式（特别是$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm系统）之间的精确对应，并实现Zagier对偶的物理实现。

Method: 基于$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和构造$U(1)^8$ Chern-Simons理论$\mathcal{T}$；利用A-twisted半指数、Wilson圈算符、粒子-涡旋对偶、gauging构造对偶理论$\mathcal{T}^\vee$；结合超共形指数、Bethe方程、TQFT结构与界面分析验证镜像对称与Zagier对偶。

Result: 确认$\mathcal{T}$与$\mathcal{T}^\vee$流向rank-zero $\mathcal{N}=4$镜像SCFT；其A/B-twisted半指数实现Zagier变换；duality interface给出$E_8$一级WZW配分函数$\\chi_{(E_8)_1}(\\tau)$。

Conclusion: Zagier对偶被具体实现为三维镜像对称与边界/界面结构的一部分，为模形式与量子场论的统一提供了新范式。

Abstract: Starting from the $(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm sum for the vacuum character of $T_{10}M_{\rm eff}(11,2)$, we construct a three-dimensional (3d) $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}$. Its $A$-twisted vacuum half-index reproduces the character exactly, while four distinguished Wilson loop half-indices agree with the remaining characters to the orders checked. Applying particle--vortex duality and gauging yields a second 3d $\mathcal N=2$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T^\vee$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}^{-1}$. Exact superconformal-index matching supports the claim that the two theories flow to 3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror SCFTs. For the $(\mathcal D,D_c)$ boundary condition, the mirror map between the $A$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T$ and the $B$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T^\vee$ coincides componentwise with the Zagier transformation, realizing a Zagier duality between the complete $(E_8,T_1)$ and $(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm systems. Using the Bethe-equation formalism, we show that the corresponding $A/B$-twisted TQFT data agree up to orientation reversal and the $E_8$ framing phase $e^{-2π\mathrm{i}/3}$. The duality interface gives the torus bilinear $Z_{\mathcal I}(τ)=\sum_iχ_i(τ)χ_i^\vee(τ)=χ_{(E_8)_1}(τ)$, where $\{χ_i\}$ and $\{χ_i^\vee\}$ are the corresponding five-component character bases. Thus Zagier duality is realized as part of a concrete 3d mirror and interface structure.

</details>


### [122] [Running soft theorems in effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08792)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Maria Derda,Riley Stewart*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子和π介子软定理在局部有效场论（EFT）框架下至一环阶的修正，发现对数型软项不受高维算符修正，而局域解析型次领头项则受硬动量区积分及Wilson系数RG方程调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解软定理在包含高维算符的有效场论中的量子修正行为，特别是区分局域解析项与对数软项对EFT参数的依赖性。

Method: 基于局部有效场论（如含Pauli算符的QED、手征EFT），在一环精度下计算光子和双软π介子散射振幅，分离硬区与软区贡献，并结合重整化群（RG）分析Wilson系数的作用。

Result: 1) 光子次领头软定理的局域解析部分受硬区积分和Pauli算符影响而被重整化；2) 对数软项在光子和π介子情形中均不受高维算符修正；3) 双软π介子定理中，对数项仅由NLSM决定，而局域次领头项系数由手征EFT四导数算符的Wilson系数及其RG方程决定。

Conclusion: 软定理的不同结构成分具有不同EFT敏感性：对数软项是‘普适’的，由低能对称性严格约束；而局域次领头项承载EFT紫外信息，其修正由RG演化所支配，为通过软过程探测量子修正提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study soft theorems for photons and pions with local effective field theory (EFT) corrections up to one loop order. In massive QED with a dimension-five Pauli operator, the local analytic part of the subleading soft photon theorem is renormalized by the hard integration region. On the other hand, as was previously known, the logarithmic soft terms do not receive corrections from higher-dimensional operators. We find that analogous structure appears in the double-soft pion theorem: the coefficients of logarithmic contributions are controlled entirely by the Nonlinear Sigma Model (NLSM) Lagrangian, while the Wilson coefficients of four-derivative operators in Chiral EFT appear in local subleading terms, in a form that is dictated by their RG equations.

</details>


### [123] [Holography in flipped AdS/$\mathbb{Z}$: Another approach to dS holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08837)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Xin-Cheng Mao,Shan-Ming Ruan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过解析延拓方法，提出了一种新的de Sitter时空全息对偶框架：将dS与一种称为'翻转AdS/ℤ'（fAdS）的时空联系起来，并构建其边界理论'翻转CFT'（fCFT），成功复现了三维纯dS和Kerr-dS宇宙视界熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统dS/CFT对应中的微妙问题，探索解析延拓作为构造de Sitter全息原理的可行路径。

Method: 建立de Sitter空间与翻转AdS/ℤ（fAdS）之间的解析延拓关系；构建fAdS的全息描述——翻转CFT（fCFT），包括外推字典和两点函数计算；利用fCFT₂的Cardy公式验证宇宙视界熵。

Result: 证实fAdS与dS可通过解析延拓关联；构建了fAdS/fCFT全息对应，两点函数与共形对称性结果一致；fCFT₂的Cardy公式精确复现了dS₃和Kerr-dS₃中宇宙视界的Bekenstein–Hawking熵。

Conclusion: fCFT可作为描述de Sitter物理的一种替代全息起点，为理解de Sitter量子引力提供了新途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the subtleties in the conventional dS/CFT correspondence, we explore analytic continuation as a constructive route to de Sitter holography. We show that quantum field theories in de Sitter space and in a spacetime that we call flipped $\mathrm{AdS}/\mathbb{Z}$ (fAdS) are related by analytic continuation. We then develop a holographic description of fAdS in terms of a boundary theory referred to as flipped CFT (fCFT). In particular, we construct the extrapolate dictionary for general asymptotically fAdS spacetimes and compute the holographic two-point functions, finding agreement with an independent derivation based on the conformal symmetry of fCFT. The analytic continuation relation between fAdS and dS further suggests that fCFT may provide a starting point for an alternative holographic description of de Sitter physics. As a nontrivial check of this picture, we show that the Cardy formula of fCFT$_2$ reproduces the Bekenstein--Hawking entropies of the cosmological horizons in both pure dS$_3$ and Kerr-dS$_3$.

</details>


### [124] [Numerical Study of Scalar Field Theory on the Fuzzy Onion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08855)
*Matej Hrmo,Samuel Kováčik,Juraj Tekel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场理论在模糊洋葱模型上的行为，使用哈密顿蒙特卡洛模拟方法分析其相结构，并与模糊球体的相结构进行比较，识别出均匀相边界和无序-非均匀相之间的临界边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量场理论在模糊洋葱模型（由半径逐渐增大的同心模糊球组成的三维矩阵模型）上的行为，以理解其相结构及动力学相变现象。

Method: 采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛数值模拟方法，分析该理论的相结构、场相特征及动力学相变，并尝试重构相变线。

Result: 识别出不同场相，发现并描述了动力学相变现象，重构了相变线，并与模糊球体的相结构进行了对比；最终确定了均匀相边界和无序-非均匀相之间的临界边界。

Conclusion: 模糊洋葱模型展现出独特的相结构特征，其相变行为既与模糊球体有相似性，也存在显著差异，特别是存在两个明确的相边界。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of the scalar field theory on the fuzzy onion model -- a three-dimensional matrix model consisting of concentric fuzzy spheres of gradually increasing radii. We use a numerical method of Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations to study the phase structure of this theory. We identify the field phases, investigate and describe a phenomenon of dynamical phase transitions and attempt to reconstruct phase transition lines. We compare the results with the well-studied phase structure of the fuzzy sphere. Finally, we identify two boundaries on the phase transitions of the theory, a uniform phase boundary and a critical boundary between the disordered and non-uniform phase.

</details>


### [125] [Production of Primordial Black Holes in the Double D3-Brane - anti-D3-Brane Inflation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08870)
*S. -H. Henry Tye*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于弦理论的双D3-反D3膜对模型，通过第一对膜对湮灭产生D1/F1弦，并利用剩余膜对的D3荷场强，在非对易几何框架下形成介电3-膜（即带D1弦的中性有限尺寸3-膜束缚态），这些介电3-膜表现为近零压物质，质量范围宽广，可作为早期宇宙原初黑洞的种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展成功的D3-反D3膜暴胀模型，引入两对D3-反D3膜以探索弦理论中新现象，特别是原初黑洞形成的机制。

Method: 基于Myers效应和非对易几何，分析D3-反D3膜对湮灭后产生的D1/F1弦与剩余膜对场强相互作用，导出介电3-膜的形成及其物理性质。

Result: 预言一类中性、有限尺寸、近零压的介电3-膜束缚态，其质量谱宽，可作为原初黑洞的种子。

Conclusion: 该模型为弦理论中原初黑洞的产生提供了新机制，将膜动力学、非对易几何与宇宙学结构形成联系起来。

Abstract: A natural extension of the successful $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane inflation model in string theory is one with two $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pairs, giving rise to a novel feature in string-theory. When the first pair annihilates, $D1$-strings and $F1$-strings are produced. Following the non-commutative geometric properties in string theory, it is shown by Myers that the presence of the $D3$-charge field strength of the remaining $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pair leads to the formation of dielectric $3$-branes -- neutral, finite-size bound states of $3$-branes with $D1$-strings. They behave as matter with little or no pressure. Spanning a wide range of masses, they seed primordial black holes in the early universe.

</details>


### [126] [Matrix Multiverses Meet Multiple Mythologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08972)
*Sidan A,Tom Banks,Willy Fischler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型，其中各种渐近德西特（dS）宇宙及其不同半径 $R_n$ 位于一个最大熵的平直 $p = ρ$ FRW 宇宙中黑洞内部；这些 dS 宇宙具有大量量子态，并在远短于 dS 回归时间的尺度上衰变并达到热平衡；该模型暗示某些自然常数或初始条件的‘不自然’取值可能源于生命存在的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何某些自然常数或宇宙初始条件呈现数学上‘不自然’的取值——可能并非偶然，而是因仅当取这些值时才能支持智能生命存在。

Method: 构建一个嵌套宇宙模型：将渐近 dS 宇宙置于平直、最大熵、满足 $p = ρ$ 的 FRW 宇宙中的黑洞内部；利用 Israel 边界条件描述壳层与 dS 视界间距 $δ_n$，估算其衰变与热化时间尺度。

Result: 推导出 dS 宇宙衰变与热化时间为 $α_n R_n \ln(R_n / δ_n)$，远短于 dS 回归时间；且内部观测者无法区分自身是否处于该嵌套结构中。

Conclusion: 该模型为‘人择原理’提供物理实现基础，表明看似不自然的宇宙参数可能是智能生命出现的必要条件，而非需额外动力学机制解释的巧合。

Abstract: We present a model in which asymptotically de Sitter universes of various types, and de Sitter (dS) radii $R_n$, live in the interiors of black holes in a maximally entropic flat $p = ρ$ Friedmann-Robertson-Walker universe. These dS universes clearly have many quantum states. We argue that they decay and equilibrate with the maximal entropy universe on time scales of order $α_n R_n {\rm ln} (R_n /δ_n)$, where $α_n$ are model-dependent dimensionless constants and $δ_n$ are the initial distances between the shell, satisfying the Israel junction conditions, and the dS horizon. These are time-scales as viewed by a detector following a trajectory far from the would-be cosmological horizon. These times are all exponentially shorter than dS recurrence times, which have no meaning in this model. It is impossible for a detector inside one of the dS universes to determine whether it is actually part of such a structure. This model could be used as the basis for claiming that certain constants of nature or cosmological initial conditions were chosen to have mathematically unnatural values because other values could not lead to any form of intelligent life.

</details>


### [127] [Superselected ghost theory: perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09017)
*Bob Holdom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于精确幽灵宇称的超选择规则，通过相似变换构造出保持幽灵宇称的微扰理论（Z₂PT），其在高阶展开中与旧式微扰理论（OFPT）有对应关系，但对跨扇区能量分母采用主值处方，并引入区分Z₂PT与OFPT的接触项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使含幽灵场的量子场论具有概率诠释，需引入基于幽灵宇称的超选择规则；但标准微扰展开不保持该宇称，因此需构造一种保持幽灵宇称的新微扰理论。

Method: 通过哈密顿量的相似变换 h = g H g⁻¹ 构造幽灵宇称守恒的微扰展开（Z₂PT），分析其与旧式微扰理论（OFPT）在三阶、四阶的对应关系，并研究跨扇区能量分母的主值处方及接触项效应。

Result: Z₂PT在三阶和四阶分别再现OFPT结果（除零分母情形外）；跨扇区能量分母采用由跃迁类型决定的主值处方；零分母导致的接触项区别于OFPT，但不改变局域初等紫外发散。

Conclusion: Z₂PT为幽灵QFT提供了严格保持幽灵宇称的微扰框架，兼容概率诠释，且在低阶与OFPT一致，同时通过主值处方和接触项体现其独特结构。

Abstract: A superselection rule based on an exact ghost parity can endow a ghost QFT with a probability interpretation. However, this ghost parity is not respected at finite order in the standard perturbative expansion. A ghost-parity-preserving perturbation theory (Z$_2$PT) is obtained through a similarity transformation of the Hamiltonian, $h=g H g^{-1}=h_0+h_1+h_2+...$. The resulting expansion is reminiscent of old-fashioned perturbation theory (OFPT) with some significant differences. The superselection rule selects the principal-value prescription for cross-sector energy denominators, with the prescription determined by the type of transition rather than the particle species. At third order, products of $h_1$ and $h_2$ combine with $h_3$ to reproduce OFPT away from vanishing denominators. At fourth order, when $h_1=0$, we show that $h_2^2$ and $h_4$ satisfy the analogous relation. Contact terms from vanishing denominators distinguish Z$_2$PT from OFPT but do not alter the local primitive UV divergences through these orders.

</details>


### [128] [Thermal Origin of Black Hole Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09797)
*D. Giataganas,G. F. Giudice,A. Kehagias,F. Quevedo,A. Riotto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出黑洞并合后产生的特征引力波（准正规模）具有精确的热力学解释：光子环附近的探针弦在世界面上产生诱导的Rindler视界，其温度由光子环的Lyapunov指数决定；准正规模由此表现为该热系统的热激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞并合后' ringing '现象（即准正规模）的物理本质，特别是其与光子环、混沌轨道及热性质之间的深层联系。

Method: 结合近光子环几何中的探针弦模型、世界面Rindler视界构造、Lyapunov指数与温度关联、以及微观涨落分析与宏观因果响应函数极点分析两种互补方法。

Result: 从微观（不稳定的横向世界面涨落）和宏观（开放热量子系统的因果响应函数极点）两个角度一致导出了黑洞准正规模谱，并确立其热激发本质。

Conclusion: 黑洞的特征铃响并非纯粹几何振动，而是源于光子环上一个具有Rindler温度的热系统对其扰动的延迟响应，为引力波谱提供了新的热力学诠释框架。

Abstract: When a black hole rings after a merger, it emits gravitational waves at characteristic frequencies known as quasinormal modes (QNMs). In the eikonal limit, these modes are governed by the unstable circular light orbits that form the photon ring. In this work, we demonstrate that the ringing of a black hole has a precise thermal interpretation. A probe string propagating in the near-ring geometry acquires an induced Rindler horizon on its worldsheet, with a temperature set by the Lyapunov exponent of the photon ring. Out of this structure, the black hole QNMs emerge as thermal excitations, so that the characteristic ringing of a black hole is the retarded response of a thermal system living on the photon ring. We explicitly derive the QNM spectrum from two complementary perspectives: microscopically, via unstable transverse worldsheet fluctuations, and macroscopically, through the pole structure of the causal response function of an open thermal quantum system.

</details>


### [129] [Long-range multipartite entanglement in holographic gapless systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09056)
*Xiantong Chen,Xuanting Ji,Xin-Xiang Ju,Wen-Peng Li,Ya-Wen Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息原理研究了三维无能隙量子系统在强耦合下的长程多体纠缠行为，发现其大距离标度指数由红外几何决定，并可通过不同红外几何实现多种标度行为（衰减、对数增长、亚体积极增长或体积律），揭示了全息无能隙相中丰富的非局部长程纠缠特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三维无能隙系统在强耦合下长程多体纠缠是否具有尺度增长行为，并在大距离处增强。

Method: 采用全息对偶方法，分析具有超缩放违反和各向异性红外几何（如AdS3×R2）的三维gapless系统中的多体纠缠标度行为。

Result: 发现一类多体纠缠量共享同一主导大距离标度指数；通过调控红外几何参数，可实现衰减、对数、亚体积极或体积律等不同标度；在AdS3×R2几何中，长程纠缠仅沿一个方向存活，横向则短程化。

Conclusion: 全息无能隙相可支持丰富且增强的长程多体纠缠，为红外物理提供了非局域表征。

Abstract: Gapless quantum systems support correlations over arbitrarily long distances, giving rise to power-law long-range entanglement. We investigate long-range entanglement at strong coupling for three-dimensional gapless systems using holography, asking whether multipartite entanglement can exhibit scale-growing behavior and become enhanced at large distances. We show that a broad class of multipartite entanglement quantities share the same leading large-distance scaling exponent determined by the IR geometry. To realize different scaling regimes, we consider hyperscaling-violating IR geometries. Depending on the parameters, long-distance multipartite entanglement can decay, become logarithmic, grow subextensively, or reach a volume law. We also analyze an anisotropic IR geometry \(\mathrm{AdS}_{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\), where long-range multipartite entanglement survives along one direction but becomes short-ranged in the transverse gapped directions. These results show that holographic gapless phases can support rich and enhanced long-range multipartite entanglement, providing a nonlocal characterization of the underlying IR physics.

</details>


### [130] [Holographic multipartite entanglement dynamics in AdS$_3$-Vaidya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09304)
*Xin-Xiang Ju,Ya-Wen Sun,Yang Zhao,Hao-Ran Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₃/CFT₂中全局猝灭后全息热化过程中多体纠缠的动态重组，发现多体纠缠的空间范围先扩展后收缩，并揭示了不同粒子数纠缠的演化时间尺度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全息热化过程中多体纠缠如何随时间动态重组，特别是不同粒子数纠缠的时空演化特征。

Method: 利用n-partite信息(-1)^n I_n、Markov gap和真正三体多熵等工具，在全息对偶框架下分析全局猝灭后的纠缠演化。

Result: 多体纠缠空间范围呈现先扩后缩行为；不同粒子数纠缠在早期以相似速率建立，但更多粒子参与的纠缠弛豫更慢；Markov gap在局域热化后仍可保持增强，而真正三体多熵则非单调演化并最终回归真空值。

Conclusion: 全局猝灭会跨空间尺度重新分配纠缠，并重构其多体结构。

Abstract: We study how multipartite entanglement is dynamically reorganized during holographic thermalization following a global quench in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$. We first use the $n$-partite information $(-1)^n I_n$ to probe collective multipartite entanglement in holographic configurations where the full $n$-region entanglement wedge is connected while all fewer-party ones are disconnected, thereby excluding fewer-party contributions. The spatial range of multipartite entanglement first expands and then contracts as the system approaches its late-time locally thermal state. Entanglement involving different numbers of parties develops on comparable early-time scales, while the entanglement that involves more parties relaxes more slowly, revealing a transient propagation from shorter to longer spatial distances. We further compute the Markov gap and the genuine tripartite multi-entropy as complementary probes of tripartite entanglement. The Markov gap can remain enhanced after local thermalization, whereas the genuine tripartite multi-entropy undergoes a nonmonotonic evolution and returns to its vacuum value for the adjacent tripartition considered in this work. These results show that a global quench redistributes the entanglement across spatial scales and reorganizes its multipartite structure.

</details>


### [131] [Nonperturbative Stabilization of D-Instantons in the Bosonic IIB Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09598)
*Yu-An Chen,Hikaru Kawai,Henry Liao,Cheng-Tsung Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB矩阵模型中D-instanton位置的非坍缩行为，指出单圈近似导致的坍缩是截断误差，真正的非坍缩是非微扰效应，已在精确双体相互作用中体现；通过U(2)模型精确计算并采用最大对角规范解决Gribov问题，发现双D-instanton间存在有限距离的稳定势阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决IIB矩阵模型中D-instanton位置在单圈有效势下坍缩至一点的长期难题，探究其是否为真实物理行为还是近似缺陷。

Method: 采用U(N)规范固定（保持对角与非对角自由度分离），辅以U(1)^N残余对称性的辅助鬼场BRST构造，发展大分离展开以实现多体力分解；精确求解U(2)模型，并对比洛伦兹规范与最大对角规范，消除Gribov歧义。

Result: U(2)模型中双D-instanton不坍缩；最大对角规范下短程力有限且排斥，双体势存在有限分离处的稳定极小值；洛伦兹规范下出现负势区域，源于Gribov问题。

Conclusion: D-instanton位置的坍缩是单圈近似的假象；非坍缩是精确双体相互作用所支持的非微扰现象，暗示完整N体系统中可能存在稳定的非坍缩分布。

Abstract: We study the bosonic type IIB (IKKT) matrix model and the fate of the D-instanton positions $p^{(i)}_μ$, the diagonal components of the $d$ Hermitian matrices, whose one-loop effective potential infamously drives them to a single point. We argue that this collapse is an artifact of the leading (one-loop) truncation, while the actual non-collapse of the $p^{(i)}_μ$ is a nonperturbative effect: it is invisible at one loop but already present in the exact (all-loop) two-body interaction. Since the two-body sector of the $N\times N$ model factorizes into copies of $N=2$, this interaction is captured exactly by the $\mathrm{U}(2)$ model, and we find that the two D-instantons do not collapse onto each other. To set up the computation, we gauge-fix the $\text{U}(N)$ symmetry in a way that keeps the diagonal and off-diagonal sectors distinct, and we handle the residual $\mathrm{U}(1)^N$ symmetry with an auxiliary-ghost BRST construction. This construction generates a new ghost four-leg vertex; the resulting Faddeev-Popov determinant admits a systematic large-separation expansion that organizes the effective potential into a many-body decomposition, separating the interaction into two-body, three-body, and higher-body contributions. The exact $N=2$ partition function is finite at finite separation; its naive Lorenz-gauge form develops a negative region at separations of order one, which we trace to a Gribov ambiguity of the Lorenz gauge and resolve with the maximal diagonal gauge--the classical frame containing the perturbative vacuum--where the short-distance force is finite and repulsive, so that the two-body potential develops a stable minimum at finite separation. These results are consistent with a stable, non-collapsed distribution of the $p^{(i)}_μ$; establishing the detailed distribution and full $N$-body non-collapse requires the higher-body potentials and is left to future work.

</details>


### [132] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory of type-B Goldstone: near-diagonal geometry and Berry term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09776)
*Pei Zheng,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了有限温度下类型B Goldstone模式的Schwinger-Keldysh有效场论，通过近对角几何解释r/a型场，并直接导出关键的Berry项，同时满足动力学KMS条件；进一步分类了陪集流形上的全局张量以构建保守、耗散与噪声部分，并在两个具体模型中计算了含耗散的Goldstone色散关系和两点关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解有限温度下类型B Goldstone模式的动力学行为，需建立兼容热力学约束（如KMS条件）且能自然包含Berry相位的有效场论框架。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh双时间路径形式，引入近对角几何诠释r/a场；利用Berry曲率的传递（transgression）导出Berry项；结合动力学KMS对称性，系统分类陪集流形上的张量以构造有效作用量各部分。

Result: 成功导出含Berry项的有限温度有效理论，验证其自动满足动力学KMS条件；给出保守、耗散与噪声项的张量分类方案；在两个具体模型中解析计算了耗散下的Goldstone色散关系和两点关联函数。

Conclusion: 该几何化Schwinger-Keldysh框架为类型B Goldstone模式提供了统一、自洽且可推广的有限温度有效描述，兼具拓扑（Berry）与非平衡（耗散/噪声）物理要素。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate a finite temperature Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory for type-B Goldstone modes. In particular, we organize the required two time contour structure by a near-diagonal geometry in which the r-type field is interpreted as the physical Goldstone configuration and the a-type field is identified as corresponding tangent displacement. Within this geometric viewpoint, the Berry term essential for type-B Goldstones is obtained directly through the transgression of the Berry curvature. Moreover, we show that this exact Berry transgression is compatible with dynamical KMS condition. In order to construct the conservative, dissipative and noise sectors, we classify possible tensors globally defined on the coset manifold. Based on this framework, we discuss in detail two concrete model examples. The dispersion relations of the Goldstone modes and the associated two-point correlation functions are calculated in the presence of dissipation.

</details>


### [133] [Closed-form thermal threshold functions for the proper-time renormalisation group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09803)
*Daniele Rizzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出有限温度下固有时间重整化群（PTRG）热阈值函数的解析表达式，通过Poisson求和将Matsubara求和转化为快速收敛的修正Bessel函数级数，并推广至任意实调节器参数，从而实现对截断方案固定点的连续追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有限温度PTRG的热阈值函数只能逐模数值计算，缺乏解析形式，限制了理论分析与调节器依赖性研究。

Method: 采用Poisson重求和技巧处理Matsubara求和，结合代数恒等式将高阶阈值函数统一归约为含位移核参数的同一闭式；分析标准单参数调节器族及尖锐固有时间调节器极限。

Result: 获得所有热阈值函数的闭式解；在LPA和LPA'截断下复现并验证已知结果；首次将零温反常维度构造推广至有限温度；实现固定点随调节器参数的连续、光滑、有界演化。

Conclusion: 本文为PTRG提供了关键解析工具，显著提升其计算效率与理论透明度，并揭示调节器依赖性的良性本质，为有限温度量子场论的非微扰研究奠定新基础。

Abstract: We derive closed-form expressions for the thermal threshold functions of the finite-temperature proper-time renormalisation group (PTRG): until now these have been evaluated numerically, Matsubara mode by Matsubara mode. For the standard one-parameter regulator family, Poisson resummation of the Matsubara sum yields a rapidly convergent winding-number series of modified Bessel functions, and a single algebraic identity reduces every higher threshold function to this same closed form at a shifted kernel parameter. The sharp proper-time regulator, recovered as the exact $m\to\infty$ endpoint of the family with a controlled $O(1/m)$ approach, factorises into a field-dependent and a purely thermal piece.
  Built on them, the local potential approximation (LPA) and its refinement to a running anomalous dimension (LPA$'$) for the $O(N)$-symmetric theory reduce to established results, all cross-checked against the exact Wetterich equation with the optimised regulator. Two findings go beyond reduction. First, the known zero-temperature anomalous-dimension construction is extended here to finite temperature. Second, because the closed form holds for any real regulator parameter, the refined truncation's fixed point can for the first time be tracked as a continuous function of the regulator, rather than at a handful of isolated points, and is found to vary smoothly and remain bounded, with no special or pathological point anywhere on the line.

</details>


### [134] [On the Renormalization in Conformal Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09858)
*Ioseph L. Buchbinder,Petr M. Lavrov,Thomas M. Sangy,Ilya L. Shapiro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了早期关于外部引力场中共形物质场的结果，应用于共形量子引力（包括Weyl平方引力及其与共形物质场的耦合），并利用BRST对称性证明了一圈阶共形不变可重整性；尽管规范固定更复杂，但一圈发散仍保持共形不变，而有限部分则产生共形反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空中共形理论的一圈发散问题在存在规范不变性时变得非平凡，因为规范固定项和鬼子作用量本身不具共形不变性，需阐明其对有效作用量共形变分的抵消机制及反常起源。

Method: 基于BRST对称性分析规范固定与鬼子扇区对有效作用量共形变分的贡献，证明其在一阶发散中的精确抵消，并将该方法推广至Weyl平方引力及共形引力与物质场耦合的情形。

Result: 确立了一圈阶共形不变可重整性；证明了发散部分满足共形不变性，而有限部分出现共形反常；给出了半经典共形引力中一圈发散共形不变性的普适证明。

Conclusion: 共形量子引力（含Weyl平方引力）在一圈近似下具有共形不变可重整性，其根源在于BRST对称性保障的规范与鬼子扇区抵消；但共形反常仍存在于有限部分，反映了量子效应破坏经典共形对称性。

Abstract: One-loop divergences in classically conformal theory in curved spacetime is a nontrivial issue if the theory under consideration possesses gauge invariance. In this case, quantization involves introducing the gauge-fixing term and the action of ghosts, both of which are not conformal. The formal proof of the conformal invariant renormalizability in this situation, including interacting theories, has been given in the paper from 1984 by one of the present authors. Owing to BRST symmetry, the contributions of the gauge-fixing and ghost sectors to the conformal variation of the effective action cancel each other. This cancellation does not hold in the finite part of the one-loop and higher-loops effective action, that results in the anomaly. In this paper, we extend the early results on conformal matter fields in external gravitational field and apply them to conformal quantum gravity, including the case of conformal gravity coupled to other conformal matter fields. The gauge-fixing in the Weyl-squared gravity is more complicated, nevertheless, the proof of the one-loop conformal invariant renormalization using BRST symmetry is possible. As a preliminary to conformal quantum gravity, we also present a detailed general proof of conformal invariant one-loop divergences in the corresponding semiclassical theory.

</details>


### [135] [Ricci-flat metrics from gauged linear $σ$-models without RG flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09918)
*Dmitri Bykov,Ondrej Hulik,Ulf Lindström,Martin Roček,Rikard von Unge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入具有不定号动能项的影子坐标（超场），利用不定号广义线性σ模型（GLSM）构造了广义圆锥上的里奇平坦凯勒度量，并给出了相应的凯勒势通式及具体例子，为卡拉比-丘度量与环面几何研究提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义圆锥上的里奇平坦凯勒度量，拓展卡拉比-丘流形与环面几何的研究框架，并理解不定号σ模型的几何意义。

Method: 基于不定号广义线性σ模型（GLSM），引入负号动能项的影子坐标（超场），推导复锥上（特别是射影空间乘积上的复锥）的显式里奇平坦凯勒度量及凯勒势通式。

Result: 得到了一类广义圆锥上 Ricci-flat Kähler 度量的显式构造，给出了通用凯勒势公式，并提供了若干具体例子；提出了广义凯勒规范化的物理解释。

Conclusion: 不定号 GLSM 可有效构造非平凡 Ricci-flat 凯勒度量，为 Calabi-Yau 几何和 toric 几何开辟新路径，并引发对不定号模型几何本质的深入思考。

Abstract: We investigate a class of Ricci-flat Kähler metrics on generalized conifolds constructed via gauged linear sigma models (GLSMs) with indefinite signature. By introducing shadow coordinates (superfields) entering the sigma model with negative signature kinetic term, we show that these GLSMs yield explicit Ricci-flat metrics on complex cones over products of projective spaces. We provide a general formula for the resulting Kähler potentials, along with detailed examples. Our results suggest new directions for the study of Calabi-Yau metrics and toric geometry, and raise interesting questions about the geometric meaning of indefinite signature models. We also give an interpretation in terms of a novel generalized Kähler gauging.

</details>


### [136] [The quadratic growth of Krylov spread complexity in the BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09922)
*Aranya Bhattacharya,Mario Flory,Michal P. Heller,Emiliano Rizza,Tim Schuhmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全息配分函数的Krylov传播复杂度构造方法，实现了对黑洞内部增长的维度无关边界重构，并在SYK模型和BTZ黑洞中验证了其有效性，发现复杂度演化呈现中间偏离、后期回归二次增长的特征，与传统体积复杂度的线性行为不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能普适地刻画黑洞内部增长（由全息复杂度表征）的边界物理量，尤其在二维以外的引力理论中尚无明确答案。

Method: 基于热场双重态的Krylov扩散复杂度的配分函数构造；结合双尺度SYK模型进行 semiclassics 与精确结果对比；应用于大中心荷二维CFT及其对偶BTZ黑洞；构建含无穷阶外曲率不变量的广义‘复杂度=任意量’体部对象。

Result: 在BTZ黑洞对偶的CFT中，复杂度早期呈二次增长，中期偏离，后期回归渐近二次行为（而非线性）；经典极限仅在复杂度重构后取才可靠；该构造可系统连接黑洞热力学与Krylov动力学，并可推广至高维全息黑洞。

Conclusion: 本文建立了维度无关的全息复杂度边界重构框架，揭示了Krylov复杂度与黑洞几何演化的深层联系，为理解黑洞内部动力学提供了新路径。

Abstract: The boundary quantity that captures the growth of black-hole interiors quantified by holographic complexity remains unknown beyond 2d dilaton gravity. We provide a critical analysis of a partition-function construction of Krylov spread complexity for thermofield-double states that provides a dimension-independent boundary reconstruction from semiclassical holographic partition functions, while developing the present dynamical and bulk construction for the BTZ saddle. In the double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, where exact and semiclassical results can be compared, we show that the classical limit is reliable only when taken after the complexity has been reconstructed; taking this limit at the level of individual Lanczos coefficients discards essential information. Applying the construction to a large-central-charge two-dimensional conformal field theory above the Hawking-Page temperature dual to a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, we find an intermediate departure from early-time quadratic growth followed by behavior compatible with a return toward asymptotically quadratic growth, rather than the linear late-time behavior of the volume and the standard finite-functional complexity = anything class. We then match this boundary behavior to a generalized complexity = anything bulk object built from an infinite series of extrinsic-curvature invariants. The construction provides a systematic route from black-hole thermodynamics to Krylov dynamics and can naturally be extended to higher-dimensional holographic black holes.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>
